        "url": "https://www.gov.br/inpe/en/events/wco5/travel-accommodation",
        "title": "Travel/Accommodation — Instituto Nacional de Pesquisas Espaciais",
        "description": "# Travel/Accommodation\n## **→ Travel to/within São José dos Campos (SJC):**\nClosest airport: [GRU Airport](https://www.gru.com.br/) – Sao Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport\n\nTransportation from GRU Airport to SJC:\n\n- Bus ( [Pássaro Marrom](https://www.passaromarron.com.br/)):\n\nThe counter opens from 6:00 a.m. to 11:00 p.m., but it is suggested to buy the ticket in advance, on the website. In case of missing the bus, the ticket can be replaced at the counter or through the app. The counter is located just outside Terminal 2 (arrivals), under the terminal roof, before crossing the street. The bus departs directly in front of the terminal, about 50 meters uphill via a small ramp, across the street.\n\nPrice: ~50,00 BRL.\n\n- Official taxis ( [Guarucoop](https://www.gru.com.br/en/passenger/to-from-gru-airport/taxi/)):\n\nDepart from all three terminals, directly in front of the arrivals area. Look for the attendant at the reception booth and request a taxi to São José dos Campos (it’s best not to respond to inquiries from other transport providers, especially those who approach you on your way out of the terminal). You will receive a small slip stating the fare, which you can pay at the end of the ride by credit card.\n\nApproximate fare: ~400 BRL.\n\nIf you arrive by Bus, it is best to get off at the SJC [intercity bus terminal](https://www.sinart.com.br/rodoviariadesaojosedoscampos?gad_source=1&gad_campaignid=22508146055&gbraid=0AAAAA-i8excBEdwHKIEq42XCnCVOue0G4&gclid=CjwKCAjwx8nCBhAwEiwA_z__08Nsnny5yr2sEgVSSrxcDmrtG181RhSatKK4myRQKbKPy2CHB0G22hoCpSwQAvD_BwE). There you will find a taxi or call an Uber to your specific destination in the city. Official taxis from the bus terminal are generally reliable, but it's a good idea to confirm the fare in advance or use apps when possible to ensure a fair price and a safe experience.\n\n[![sjc1.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc1.png/@@images/8bd7349a-6dd3-4a77-87bc-00d3379c5396.png)](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc1.png)Transporte e Hotéis\n\n**Public Transport in SJC:**\n\nTo check bus schedules, you can use apps like Moovit or visit the website: [[https://www.sjc.sp.gov.br/servicos/mobilidade-urbana/novos-horarios-do-transporte-publico/](https://www.sjc.sp.gov.br/servicos/mobilidade-urbana/novos-horarios-do-transporte-publico/)](https://www.sjc.sp.gov.br/servicos/mobilidade-urbana/novos-horarios-do-transporte-publico/)\n\n**First Arrival at INPE:**\n\n![sjc2.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc2.png/@@images/25ac1db6-e291-4e4d-8ee8-b2b106651a62.png)Mapa aéreo INPE![sjc3.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/en/events/wco5/image-repository/sjc3.png/@@images/58349484-a401-4a3c-bb8d-a337f4c4c7b0.png)Mapa aéreo INPE 2\n\nMap: [[https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/298019#map=18/-23.209895/-45.859243](https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/298019#map=18/-23.209895/-45.859243)](https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/298019#map=18/-23.209895/-45.859243)\n\n## **→ Weather and climate in São José dos Campos (March):**\nEvenings can be slightly cooler, so a light long-sleeve shirt might be useful.\n\nAdvanced cookie settings\n\n[See Cookies Statement](https://www.gov.br/pt-br/termos-de-uso)\n\n**By selecting \"Reject Cookies\", those that are not strictly necessary will be disabled**. Learn more in our [Cookie Statement.](https://www.gov.br/pt-br/termos-de-uso)\n\nStrictly necessary cookies\n\nDesselecionar toda classe\n\n**These cookies cannot be disabled on our systems.**\n\nCookies\n\nlgpd-cookie-v2\n\nDue date\n\n365 days\n\nDomain\n\n[www.gov.br](http://www.gov.br)\n\nCompany\n\nGovbr\n\nGoal\n\nFunctional /         "markdown": "Info\n\n# Travel/Accommodation\n\nPublished inJul 07, 2025 10:11 AMUpdated inMar 02, 2026 07:27 AM\n\n## **→ Travel to/within São José dos Campos (SJC):**\n\nClosest airport: [GRU Airport](https://www.gru.com.br/ \"\") – Sao Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport\n\nTransportation from GRU Airport to SJC:\n\n- Bus ( [Pássaro Marrom](https://www.passaromarron.com.br/ \"\")):\n\nThe counter opens from 6:00 a.m. to 11:00 p.m., but it is suggested to buy the ticket in advance, on the website. In case of missing the bus, the ticket can be replaced at the counter or through the app. The counter is located just outside Terminal 2 (arrivals), under the terminal roof, before crossing the street. The bus departs directly in front of the terminal, about 50 meters uphill via a small ramp, across the street.\n\nPrice: ~50,00 BRL.\n- Official taxis ( [Guarucoop](https://www.gru.com.br/en/passenger/to-from-gru-airport/taxi/ \"\")):\n\nDepart from all three terminals, directly in front of the arrivals area. Look for the attendant at the reception booth and request a taxi to São José dos Campos (it’s best not to respond to inquiries from other transport providers, especially those who approach you on your way out of the terminal). You will receive a small slip stating the fare, which you can pay at the end of the ride by credit card.\n\nApproximate fare: ~400 BRL.\n\nIf you arrive by Bus, it is best to get off at the SJC [intercity bus terminal](https://www.sinart.com.br/rodoviariadesaojosedoscampos?gad_source=1&gad_campaignid=22508146055&gbraid=0AAAAA-i8excBEdwHKIEq42XCnCVOue0G4&gclid=CjwKCAjwx8nCBhAwEiwA_z__08Nsnny5yr2sEgVSSrxcDmrtG181RhSatKK4myRQKbKPy2CHB0G22hoCpSwQAvD_BwE \"\"). There you will find a taxi or call an Uber to your specific destination in the city. While both Uber and official taxis are commonly used in SJC, each has its considerations. If you choose Uber, we recommend prioritizing the Comfort option. Official taxis from the bus terminal are generally reliable, but it's a good idea to confirm the fare in advance or use apps when possible to ensure a fair price and a safe experience.\n\n[![sjc1.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc1.png/@@images/8bd7349a-6dd3-4a77-87bc-00d3379c5396.png)](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc1.png)Transporte e Hotéis\n\n**Public Transport in SJC:**\n\nTo check bus schedules, you can use apps like Moovit or visit the website: [https://www.sjc.sp.gov.br/servicos/mobilidade-urbana/novos-horarios-do-transporte-publico/](https://www.sjc.sp.gov.br/servicos/mobilidade-urbana/novos-horarios-do-transporte-publico/ \"\")\n\n**First Arrival at INPE:**\n\nINPE has four gates, but visitors should enter through the main gate, located at Av. dos Astronautas, 1,758 – Jardim da Granja, São José dos Campos – SP, 12227-010.\n\nBe sure to bring a valid ID and inform the security guard about your visit.\n\n![sjc2.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/pt-br/eventos/wco5/repositorio-de-imagens/sjc2.png/@@images/25ac1db6-e291-4e4d-8ee8-b2b106651a62.png)Mapa aéreo INPE![sjc3.png](https://www.gov.br/inpe/en/events/wco5/image-repository/sjc3.png/@@images/58349484-a401-4a3c-bb8d-a337f4c4c7b0.png)Mapa aéreo INPE 2\n\nMap: [https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/298019#map=18/-23.209895/-45.859243](https://www.openstreetmap.org/relation/298019#map=18/-23.209895/-45.859243 \"\")\n\n## **→** **Accommodations in SJC:**\n\nWe recommend staying in the neighborhoods of Esplanada, Vila Ema, Vila Adyanna, Jardim Aquarius, or Colinas. SJC’s city center is mostly commercial and not the most pleasant area to stay in. The neighborhood near INPE might be a convenient, walkable location, but it has limited options for restaurants, hotels, and leisure.\n\n**Some hotel recommendations are:**\n\n- [Nacional Inn](https://www.nacionalinn.com.br/hoteis/hotel-nacional-inn-sao-jose-dos-campos \"\")\n\n- [Mercure](https://all.accor.com/hotel/5168/index.pt.shtml \"\")\n\n- [Ema Palace](https://www.hotelemapalace.com.br/ \"\")\n\n- [Marua Hotel](http://www.hotelmarua.com.br/ \"\")\n\n\n## **→ Leisure Options in SJC:**\n\nThe main shopping malls in the city are CenterVale, Vale Sul, and Colinas.\n\nLocals often enjoy going to parks and participating in various cultural activities. The main parks are Parque da Cidade, Vicentina Aranha, Santos Dumont, and Alberto Simões.\n\nIn the surrounding area, there are many beautiful sightseeing spots in the Mantiqueira mountain range, including the towns of São Francisco Xavier, Santo Antônio do Pinhal and Campos do Jordão. On the coast, popular beach destinations such as São Sebastião, Ilhabela, Caraguatatuba, and Ubatuba are within reach. The city of São Paulo is approximately 1 hour and 30 minutes away by bus.\n\n## **→ Weather and climate in São José dos Campos (March):**\n\nMarch in São Paulo state marks the transition from summer to autumn, offering a warm and humid climate.\n\n- Temperature: Expect average high temperatures around 27-30°C (80-86°F) and average lows around 13-18°C (55-64°F). Days are generally warm to moderately hot, but the high humidity can make it feel warmer.\n- Rainfall: March is typically one of the wettest months. Be prepared for frequent rain, with around 11 rainy days in March on average and monthly rainfall averaging 133mm, often in the form of afternoon thunderstorms. An umbrella and light rain gear are highly recommended.\n- Humidity: High humidity is common, averaging 80%.\n- Sunshine: You can expect around 5 hours of bright sunshine per day.\n- Wind: Winds are generally light, offering a gentle breeze.\n\n**Recommendations**: Overall, pack light, breathable clothing, and definitely include rain and sun protection. Evenings can be slightly cooler, so a light long-sleeve shirt might be useful.\n\nAdvanced cookie settings\n\n[See Cookies Statement](https://www.gov.br/pt-br/termos-de-uso)\n\nTo improve your experience on the platform and provide personalized services, we use cookies.\n\nTo improve your experience on the platform and provide personalized services, we use cookies. **By accepting, you will be able to use all the site's functionalities. By selecting \"Reject Cookies\", those that are not strictly necessary will be disabled**. To choose which ones you want to authorize, select \"Manage Cookies\". Learn more in our [Cookie Statement.](https://www.gov.br/pt-br/termos-de-uso)\n\nStrictly necessary cookies\n\nDesselecionar toda classe\n\nThese cookies enable essential functionality such as security, identity verification and network management. **These cookies cannot be disabled on our systems.** Although they are necessary, you can block these cookies directly in your browser, but         "url": "https://www.icsm2026.com/arriving-in-brazil/",
        "title": "Arriving in Brazil - ICSM 2026",
        "description": "## Arriving in Brazil\n[View the official Uber pickup guide at Galeão Airport](https://www.icsm2026.com/official-uber-pickup/)\n\nBrazil is well connected to major international destinations through a wide network of airports. The main gateways into the country include Rio de Janeiro’s Antonio Carlos Jobim International Airport, commonly known as Galeão (GIG), São Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport (GRU), and Campinas–Viracopos International Airport (VCP). Both São Paulo and Campinas offer multiple daily flights to and from Rio de Janeiro.\n\n#### Airports in Rio de Janeiro and Transportation to the Convention Centre and Hotels\nGaleão International Airport (GIG) is located approximately 38 km from the Windsor Convention & Expo Center via the expressway. Depending on traffic conditions and route selection, transfer times typically range from 35 to 70 minutes.\n\n![Map](https://www.icsm2026.com/image/map1.jpg)Image Source: Google Maps\n\nSantos Dumont Airport (SDU) in Rio de Janeiro operates exclusively domestic flights, serving destinations within approximately 500 km of the city. Its prime location in downtown Rio makes it a convenient option for travelers. Transfer times to the convention center typically range from 35 to 70 minutes, depending on traffic conditions.\n\nFor travelers arriving in Brazil via São Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport (GRU), connecting to Santos Dumont Airport (SDU) may be more convenient than flying into Galeão Airport (GIG). SDU is known for offering breathtaking views during both takeoff and landing.\n\nHowever, if you land at GRU and choose to connect to SDU, **be sure not to book a connecting flight departing from Congonhas Airport (CGH)**, located in downtown São Paulo. Doing so would require traveling between GRU and CGH by bus or car, a journey that can be lengthy due to heavy traffic. This situation is similar to arriving at New York’s JFK Airport and then needing to transfer to LaGuardia Airport for your next flight.\n\n![Map](https://www.icsm2026.com/image/map2.jpg)Image Source: Google Maps\n\nOfficial yellow taxis and Uber are widely available and reliable transportation options in Rio de Janeiro. Dedicated taxi services also operate at the airport. Public transportation is available throughout the city, though its efficiency varies by area. **Note that there are no metro or train lines directly connecting Galeão Airport to the rest of the city.** In contrast, Santos Dumont Airport offers convenient access to both the subway and commuter rail via a short ride on the light rail (VLT).\n\nA new multimodal transportation hub, the Kindness Terminal (“Terminal Gentileza”), was recently inaugurated in downtown Rio. An express bus line now links Rio de Janeiro International Airport to this terminal, running every 20 minutes in both directions. **The terminal is integrated with the VLT system**, providing seamless connections to Santos Dumont Airport, major subway stations, and popular attractions such as the Museum of Tomorrow, the Rio Aquarium, and the Rio Museum of Art. Although this route offers scenic views and easy access to cultural sites, it may not be the fastest option for airport transfers.\n\n#### Recommended Transfer options\n##### **Option 1: Booked Transfers (Recommended)**\n- Prices are valid for the conference and correspond to one way transfer Airport to Hotel or Hotel to Airport.\n\n- **Van: BRL 1,200.00**\n\n##### **Option 2: Regular Official Taxi**\n**Official Airport Taxi – yellow taxi (Recommended).** Up to 3 people, depending on the luggage size:\n\n- Estimated cost of the ride (GIG-Convention Center): ~USD 32.\n- Estimated cost of the ride (SDU-Convention Center): ~USD 26.\n\nSeveral official taxicab booths are also located in the same area. Always prefer the pre-paid fares. You may choose between the Yellow Cab or one of the Special Taxi companies. Credit cards are accepted.\n\nIf you are familiar with Brazil and Rio, you may exit the arrival room and pick a taxi at the taxi waiting line (just outside) and pay the ride marked on the taximeter (counter). Just be sure to pick an official Yellow Airport Taxi.\n\n**Important:** No tips are needed for Uber and Taxi in Rio de Janeiro. If you wish to give a tip it is never more than 10%. On the other hand, taxis are allowed to charge a fixed value per each luggage volume. The value is added to the taximeter (counter) value.\n\n##### **Option 3: Uber (only Black is recommended)**\n**Estimated cost of the ride: USD 25 – 34**\n\n- values depending on the car category and traffic conditions;\n- up to 3 people, depending on the size of luggage pieces.\n\nUber boarding points are well signed at and outside the GIG arrival rooms. Follow the instructions.",
        "position": 8,
        "markdown": "[Register here / Log in / Submission](https://www.eventweb.com.br/icsm2026/ \"Register here / Log in / Submission\")\n\n## Arriving in Brazil\n\n[View the official Uber pickup guide at Galeão Airport](https://www.icsm2026.com/official-uber-pickup/)\n\nBrazil is well connected to major international destinations through a wide network of airports. The main gateways into the country include Rio de Janeiro’s Antonio Carlos Jobim International Airport, commonly known as Galeão (GIG), São Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport (GRU), and Campinas–Viracopos International Airport (VCP). Both São Paulo and Campinas offer multiple daily flights to and from Rio de Janeiro.\n\n#### Airports in Rio de Janeiro and Transportation to the Convention Centre and Hotels\n\nGaleão International Airport (GIG) is located approximately 38 km from the Windsor Convention & Expo Center via the expressway. Depending on traffic conditions and route selection, transfer times typically range from 35 to 70 minutes.\n\n![Map](https://www.icsm2026.com/image/map1.jpg)Image Source: Google Maps\n\nSantos Dumont Airport (SDU) in Rio de Janeiro operates exclusively domestic flights, serving destinations within approximately 500 km of the city. Its prime location in downtown Rio makes it a convenient option for travelers. Transfer times to the convention center typically range from 35 to 70 minutes, depending on traffic conditions.\n\nFor travelers arriving in Brazil via São Paulo/Guarulhos International Airport (GRU), connecting to Santos Dumont Airport (SDU) may be more convenient than flying into Galeão Airport (GIG). SDU is known for offering breathtaking views during both takeoff and landing.\n\nHowever, if you land at GRU and choose to connect to SDU, **be sure not to book a connecting flight departing from Congonhas Airport (CGH)**, located in downtown São Paulo. Doing so would require traveling between GRU and CGH by bus or car, a journey that can be lengthy due to heavy traffic. This situation is similar to arriving at New York’s JFK Airport and then needing to transfer to LaGuardia Airport for your next flight.\n\n![Map](https://www.icsm2026.com/image/map2.jpg)Image Source: Google Maps\n\nOfficial yellow taxis and Uber are widely available and reliable transportation options in Rio de Janeiro. Dedicated taxi services also operate at the airport. Public transportation is available throughout the city, though its efficiency varies by area. **Note that there are no metro or train lines directly connecting Galeão Airport to the rest of the city.** In contrast, Santos Dumont Airport offers convenient access to both the subway and commuter rail via a short ride on the light rail (VLT).\n\nA new multimodal transportation hub, the Kindness Terminal (“Terminal Gentileza”), was recently inaugurated in downtown Rio. An express bus line now links Rio de Janeiro International Airport to this terminal, running every 20 minutes in both directions. **The terminal is integrated with the VLT system**, providing seamless connections to Santos Dumont Airport, major subway stations, and popular attractions such as the Museum of Tomorrow, the Rio Aquarium, and the Rio Museum of Art. Although this route offers scenic views and easy access to cultural sites, it may not be the fastest option for airport transfers.\n\n#### Recommended Transfer options\n\n##### **Option 1: Booked Transfers (Recommended)**\n\n- Organized with the partner Travel Agency Follow Up Tours and Events\n- Prices are valid for the conference and correspond to one way transfer Airport to Hotel or Hotel to Airport.\n\n**Fixed Rates for the ICSM 2026 Congress:**\n\n- **Sedan: BRL 375.00**\n\n\nNote: up to 3 people with small luggage. If large luggage: 2 people per car.\n- **Van: BRL 1,200.00**\n\n\nNote: up to 9 people with small luggage. If large luggage: max 8 people per van.\n\nTo book your transfer with the Official Travel Agency of ICSM 2026 (Follow Up):\n\n[https://www.net-expert.com/followuptour/Evento/icsm2026](https://www.net-expert.com/followuptour/Evento/icsm2026)\n\n**Note:** If you have any question or need any assistance, please contact\n[cristianecherulli@followuptour.com.br](mailto:cristianecherulli@followuptour.com.br)\n\nOr by WhatsApp: +55 21 99803-4854\n\n\n##### **Option 2: Regular Official Taxi**\n\n**Official Airport Taxi – yellow taxi (Recommended).** Up to 3 people, depending on the luggage size:\n\n- Estimated cost of the ride (GIG-Convention Center): ~USD 32.\n- Estimated cost of the ride (SDU-Convention Center): ~USD 26.\n\nSeveral official taxicab booths are also located in the same area. Always prefer the pre-paid fares. You may choose between the Yellow Cab or one of the Special Taxi companies. Credit cards are accepted.\n\nIf you are familiar with Brazil and Rio, you may exit the arrival room and pick a taxi at the taxi waiting line (just outside) and pay the ride marked on the taximeter (counter). Just be sure to pick an official Yellow Airport Taxi.\n\n**Important:** No tips are needed for Uber and Taxi in Rio de Janeiro. If you wish to give a tip it is never more than 10%. On the other hand, taxis are allowed to charge a fixed value per each luggage volume. The value is added to the taximeter (counter) value.\n\n##### **Option 3: Uber (only Black is recommended)**\n\n**Estimated cost of the ride: USD 25 – 34**\n\n- values depending on the car category and traffic conditions;\n- up to 3 people, depending on the size of luggage pieces.\n\nUber boarding points are well signed at and outside the GIG arrival rooms. Follow the instructions.",
